Units: I is in K; $Cp^{\circ}$ and $S^{\circ}$ are in cal/mol·K; $H^{\circ} - H_{298}^{\circ}$ , $\Delta Hf^{\circ}$ and $\Delta Gf^{\circ}$ are in kcal/mol.
